   1++6F68              ; ============================================================================
   2++6F68              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6F68              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6F68              ;
   5++6F68              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6F68              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6F68              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6F68              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6F68              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6F68              ; ============================================================================
  11++6F68              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6F68              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6F68              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6F68              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6F68              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6F68              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6F68
  18++6F68              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6F68                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6F68 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6F6B 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6F6E B7                   or      a
  23++6F6F ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6F71 C2 B0 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6F74
  26++6F74                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6F74 21 88 95             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6F77 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6F7A B7                   or      a
  30++6F7B ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6F7D DA B0 6F             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6F80 CA B0 6F             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6F83 22 CA 6F             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6F86 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6F87 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6F89 D2 B0 6F             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6F8C
  38++6F8C 21 88 95             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6F8F 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6F92 B7                   or      a
  41++6F93 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6F95 DA 9E 6F             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6F98 CA 9E 6F             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6F9B C3 B0 6F             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6F9E              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6F9E                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6F9E 3A 7E 75             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6FA1 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6FA3 C2 B0 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6FA6 3A 7B 75             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6FA9 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6FAB C2 B0 6F             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6FAE B7                   or      a
  54++6FAF C9                   ret
  55++6FB0
  56++6FB0              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6FB0 37                   scf
  58++6FB1 C9                   ret
  59++6FB2
  60++6FB2              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6FB2              p127_layout_report:

 480+ 7004                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++7004              ; ============================================================================
   2++7004              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++7004              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++7004              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++7004              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++7004              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++7004              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++7004              ; ============================================================================
   9++7004              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++7004              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++7004              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++7004
  13++7004              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++7004              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++7004              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++7004              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++7004              p127_resident_start:
  18++7004
  19++7004              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++7004              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++7004              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++7004              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++7004              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++7004              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++7004
  26++7004              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++7004              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++7004              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++7004              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++7004              p127_resident_validate:
  31++7004 32 8E 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++7007 78                   ld      a,b
  33++7008 32 8F 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++700B 79                   ld      a,c
  35++700C 32 90 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++700F 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++7010 32 91 70             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++7013
  39++7013 3A 8E 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++7016 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++7018 C2 42 70             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++701B 3A 8F 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++701E FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++7020 C2 42 70             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++7023 3A 90 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++7026 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++7028 C2 42 70             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++702B 3A 91 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++702E E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++7030 CA 42 70             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++7033                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++7033 3A 8E 70             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++7036 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++7038 CA 42 70             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++703B FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++703D CA 42 70             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++7040 B7                   or      a
  58++7041 C9                   ret
  59++7042
  60++7042              p127_resident_reject:
  61++7042 37                   scf
  62++7043 C9                   ret
  63++7044
  64++7044              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++7044              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++7044              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++7044 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++7046 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++7048 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++704A 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++704C CD 04 70             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++704F DA 8C 70             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++7052
  74++7052 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++7054 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++7056 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++7058 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++705A CD 04 70             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++705D D2 8C 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++7060
  81++7060 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++7062 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++7064 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++7066 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++7068 CD 04 70             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++706B D2 8C 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++706E
  88++706E 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++7070 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++7072 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++7074 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++7076 CD 04 70             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++7079 D2 8C 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++707C
  95++707C 3E 03                ld      a,3

  99++7084 CD 04 70             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++7087 D2 8C 70             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
 101++708A B7                   or      a
 102++708B C9                   ret

 104++708C              p127_resident_test_fail:
 105++708C 37                   scf
 106++708D C9                   ret
 107++708E
 108++708E              p127_resident_arg_target:
 109++708E 00                   db      0
 110++708F              p127_resident_arg_code:
 111++708F 00                   db      0
 112++7090              p127_resident_arg_stack:
 113++7090 00                   db      0
 114++7091              p127_resident_arg_flags:
 115++7091 00                   db      0
 116++7092
 117++7092
 118++7092              ; W3 ownership / UI quiesce state machine. Software only; no page OUT.
 119++7092              P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE EQU 1
 120++7092              P127_W3_STATE_QUIESCED EQU 2
 121++7092              P127_W3_OWNER_UI EQU 1
 122++7092              P127_W3_OWNER_NONE EQU 0
 123++7092              P127_W3_OWNER_HANDOFF EQU 2
 124++7092
 125++7092              p127_w3_init:
 126++7092 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 127++7094 32 2F 72             ld      (p127_w3_state),a
 128++7097 3E 01                ld      a,P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 129++7099 32 30 72             ld      (p127_w3_owner),a
 130++709C AF                   xor     a
 131++709D 32 31 72             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 132++70A0 B7                   or      a
 133++70A1 C9                   ret
 134++70A2
 135++70A2              p127_w3_ui_enter:
 136++70A2 3A 2F 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 137++70A5 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 138++70A7 20 79                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 139++70A9 3A 30 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 140++70AC FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 141++70AE 20 72                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 142++70B0 3A 31 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_busy)
 143++70B3 B7                   or      a
 144++70B4 20 6C                jr      nz,p127_w3_reject
 145++70B6 3E 01                ld      a,1
 146++70B8 32 31 72             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 147++70BB B7                   or      a
 148++70BC C9                   ret
 149++70BD
 150++70BD              p127_w3_ui_leave:
 151++70BD AF                   xor     a
 152++70BE 32 31 72             ld      (p127_w3_busy),a
 153++70C1 C9                   ret
 154++70C2
 155++70C2              p127_w3_quiesce:
 156++70C2 3A 2F 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 157++70C5 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE

 348++7235              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 349++7235              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++7235              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++7235              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++7235              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++7235              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++7235              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++7235              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.
 356++7235              p127_resident_trampoline_preflight:

 358++7236                      ; Linked code and data must remain wholly inside the fixed W1 window.
 359++7236 21 04 70             ld      hl,p127_resident_start
 360++7239 11 00 60             ld      de,06000h
 361++723C B7                   or      a
 362++723D ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 363++723F DA 82 72             jp      c,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 364++7242
 365++7242 21 84 72             ld      hl,p127_resident_end
 366++7245 11 00 80             ld      de,08000h
 367++7248 B7                   or      a
 368++7249 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 369++724B D2 82 72             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 370++724E
 371++724E                      ; Representative mutable state must be within the resident module.

 374++7254 B7                   or      a
 375++7255 ED 52                sbc     hl,de

 377++725A

 380++7260 B7                   or      a
 381++7261 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
 382++7263 D2 82 72             jp      nc,p127_resident_trampoline_fail
 383++7266
 384++7266                      ; CALL has already pushed its return address. The live stack must
 385++7266                      ; still be within W2, independent of W3's mapping.
 386++7266 21 00 00             ld      hl,0
 387++7269 39                   add     hl,sp
 388++726A 11 00 B0             ld      de,0B000h
 389++726D B7                   or      a
 390++726E ED 52                sbc     hl,de

   2++7284              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7284              ; Module version: 0.4.4
   4++7284              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7284              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7284              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7284              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7284              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7284              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7284              ; ============================================================================

   2++75F9              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++75F9              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++75F9              ; ABI:
   5++75F9              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++75F9              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++75F9              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL

   1++7713              ; ============================================================================
   2++7713              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++7713              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++7713              ;
   5++7713              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++7713              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++7713              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++7713              ;
   9++7713              ; API:
  10++7713              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++7713              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++7713              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++7713              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++7713              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++7713              ;
  16++7713              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++7713              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++7713              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++7713              ; ============================================================================
  20++7713              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++7713              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++7713              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++7713              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++7713              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++7713
  26++7713              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++7713              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++7713              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++7713              p127_arena_init:

  37++771F 2B                   dec     hl

  39++7721 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++7723 C3 D9 77             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++7726              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:

  50++7736 32 6B 78             ld      (p127_arena_page_count),a
  51++7739 3E 40                ld      a,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  52++773B 32 6C 78             ld      (p127_arena_next_page),a
  53++773E B7                   or      a
  54++773F C9                   ret
  55++7740

   1++7871              ; ============================================================================
   2++7871              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++7871              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7871              ;
   5++7871              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++7871              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++7871              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++7871              ;
   9++7871              ; API:
  10++7871              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++7871              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++7871              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++7871              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++7871              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++7871              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++7871              ; ============================================================================
  17++7871              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++7871              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++7871              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  20++7871              P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT EQU 256
  21++7871              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h
  22++7871              P127_AS_UNMAPPED EQU 0FFh
  23++7871
  24++7871              p127_as_init:
  25++7871 21 12 7A             ld      hl,p127_as_page_table
  26++7874 36 FF                ld      (hl),P127_AS_UNMAPPED
  27++7876 11 13 7A             ld      de,p127_as_page_table+1
  28++7879 01 FF 00             ld      bc,P127_AS_LOGICAL_PAGE_COUNT-1

  30++787E B7                   or      a
  31++787F C9                   ret

  33++7880              ; Bind an allocator-owned physical page. Physical aliases are forbidden.

   5++7B12              ; Metadata only: no byte access/copy, mapping, OUT, DMA, IRQ, IM or EI.

   1++7CF2              ; ============================================================================
   2++7CF2              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7CF2              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7CF2              ;
   5++7CF2              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7CF2              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7CF2              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7CF2              ;
   9++7CF2              ; API inputs:
  10++7CF2              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7CF2              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7CF2              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7CF2              ; Outputs:
  14++7CF2              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7CF2              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7CF2              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7CF2              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7CF2              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7CF2              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7CF2              ; ============================================================================
  21++7CF2              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7CF2              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7CF2              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7CF2              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7CF2              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7CF2
  27++7CF2              p127_as_copy:
  28++7CF2 AF                   xor     a

 311++7F18              ; IN HL=left uint32 pointer, DE=right uint32 pointer.
 312++7F18              ; OUT carry if left<right; Z if equal. Preserves BC.
 313++7F18              p127_as_copy_compare_u32:
 314++7F18 C5                   push    bc

 338++7F34 C9                   ret

   1++8631              ; ============================================================================
   2++8631              ; Project127 TR-DOS directory reader
   3++8631              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++8631              ; Reads track 0 sectors 1..8 via the documented TR-DOS ROM entry #3D13.
   5++8631              ; Directory snapshot: 128 * 16 bytes at #C800 (reserved host/staging page P0).
   6++8631              ; Read-only: no file-data reads, writes, DMA, IM/EI or raw paging OUT.
   7++8631              ; ============================================================================
   8++8631              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9++8631              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  10++8631              P127_TRDOS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  11++8631              P127_TRDOS_DIRECTORY_BUFFER EQU 0C800h
  12++8631              ; Separate from the live caller stack at #BFFF and handoff probe stack #B800.
  13++8631              ; The 240-byte usable interval #B700..#B7EF is below the handoff canary.
  14++8631              P127_TRDOS_SCRATCH_STACK_TOP EQU 0B7F0h
  15++8631              P127_TRDOS_DIRECTORY_BYTES EQU 0800h
  16++8631              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_COUNT EQU 128
  17++8631              P127_TRDOS_ENTRY_SIZE EQU 16
  18++8631
  19++8631              ; Public entry. Returns carry set on sector-read error.
  20++8631              ; Output is mirrored by p127_puts/p127_put_char to the visible console and #EF.
  21++8631              ; Called immediately after the Project126-compatible legacy mapping
  22++8631              ; setup, before p127_ui_init changes the ROM/video/shadow-port state.

  60++8676              ; accepts a zero-based sector in E and internally selects physical sector E+1.

# file opened: p127_speaker.asm
   1++8B7E              ; ============================================================================
   2++8B7E              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8B7E              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8B7E              ;
   5++8B7E              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8B7E              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8B7E              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8B7E              ;
   9++8B7E              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8B7E              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8B7E              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8B7E              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8B7E              ;
  14++8B7E              ; API:
  15++8B7E              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8B7E              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8B7E              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8B7E              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8B7E              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8B7E              ;
  21++8B7E              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8B7E              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8B7E              ; is assumed.
  24++8B7E              ; ============================================================================
  25++8B7E              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8B7E              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8B7E              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8B7E
  29++8B7E              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8B7E              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8B7E              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8B7E
  33++8B7E              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8B7E E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8B80 32 DC 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++8B83 C9                   ret
  37++8B84
  38++8B84              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8B84              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8B84              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8B84              p127_speaker_set:
  42++8B84 C5                   push    bc
  43++8B85 47                   ld      b,a
  44++8B86 3A DC 8B             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++8B89 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++8B8B 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++8B8C 78                   ld      a,b
  48++8B8D E6 01                and     1
  49++8B8F 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++8B91 79                   ld      a,c
  51++8B92 F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++8B94 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++8B96              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++8B96 79                   ld      a,c
  55++8B97              p127_speaker_write:
  56++8B97 32 DC 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++8B9A D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++8B9C C1                   pop     bc
  59++8B9D C9                   ret
  60++8B9E
  61++8B9E              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8B9E              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++8B9E 3A DC 8B             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++8BA1 EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++8BA3 32 DC 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++8BA6 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8BA8 C9                   ret
  68++8BA9
  69++8BA9              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++8BA9              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++8BA9 3A DC 8B             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++8BAC E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++8BAE 32 DC 8B             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++8BB1 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8BB3 C9                   ret
  76++8BB4
  77++8BB4              p127_speaker_get:
  78++8BB4 3A DC 8B             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++8BB7 C9                   ret
  80++8BB8
  81++8BB8
  82++8BB8              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++8BB8              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8BB8              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++8BB8              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++8BB8              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++8BB8 F5                   push    af
  88++8BB9 C5                   push    bc
  89++8BBA D5                   push    de
  90++8BBB E5                   push    hl
  91++8BBC E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++8BBE CD 7E 8B             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++8BC1 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++8BC4              p127_speaker_test_loop:
  95++8BC4 CD 9E 8B             call    p127_speaker_toggle
  96++8BC7 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
  97++8BC9              p127_speaker_test_delay1:
  98++8BC9 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay1
  99++8BCB 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
 100++8BCD              p127_speaker_test_delay2:
 101++8BCD 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay2
 102++8BCF 1B                   dec     de
 103++8BD0 7A                   ld      a,d
 104++8BD1 B3                   or      e
 105++8BD2 20 F0                jr      nz,p127_speaker_test_loop
 106++8BD4 CD A9 8B             call    p127_speaker_stop
 107++8BD7 E1                   pop     hl
 108++8BD8 D1                   pop     de
 109++8BD9 C1                   pop     bc
 110++8BDA F1                   pop     af
 111++8BDB C9                   ret
 112++8BDC
 113++8BDC 00           p127_speaker_shadow: db 0
 114++8BDD

   2++8EB3              ; Project127 FAT16 root-directory reader
   3++8EB3              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8EB3              ; Derived from Project127/113 FAT16 BPB/root-directory flow.
   5++8EB3              ; Uses the existing NEMO-DIVIDE LBA28 single-sector transport in p127_hdd.asm.
   6++8EB3              ; Read-only: no writes, DMA, IRQ, IM/EI or paging OUT.
   7++8EB3              ; Output: each root entry is mirrored to the ATM console and debug port #EF.
   8++8EB3              ; ============================================================================

  10++8EB3              P127_HDD_FAT16_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1

  14++8EB3              ; read every root-directory sector and print each valid 8.3 entry.
  15++8EB3              ; IN: P127_HDD_SECTOR_BUFFER contains the partition boot sector from probe.
  16++8EB3              ; OUT: carry=1 on invalid BPB or sector-read failure.

   1++91D1              ; ============================================================================
   2++91D1              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++91D1              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++91D1              ; This is a DI-only bootstrap with a masked IM2 vector installer.
   5++91D1              ; IM2/vector setup is allowed; EI and hardware page OUT remain forbidden.
   6++91D1              ; ============================================================================
   7++91D1              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++91D1              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++91D1              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++91D1              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++91D1              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++91D1              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++91D1
  14++91D1              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++91D1              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++91D1              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++91D1              ; The boot installer may establish the vector, mapping and handler ownership
  18++91D1              ; tuple while DI remains asserted. Interrupts stay disabled until a separate
  19++91D1              ; controlled source/acknowledge test validates live delivery and RETI.
  20++91D1              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  21++91D1              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  22++91D1              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  23++91D1              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  24++91D1
  25++91D1              p127_irq_init:
  26++91D1 F3                   di
  27++91D2 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  28++91D4 32 84 95             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  29++91D7 AF                   xor     a
  30++91D8 32 85 95             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  31++91DB 32 86 95             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  32++91DE 32 87 95             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  33++91E1 B7                   or      a
  34++91E2 C9                   ret
  35++91E3
  36++91E3              ; OUT: carry clear only while the documented DI-only/no-vector contract holds.
  37++91E3              p127_irq_validate:
  38++91E3 3A 84 95             ld      a,(p127_irq_mask_state)
  39++91E6 FE 01                cp      P127_IRQ_MASKED
  40++91E8 C2 F7 93             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  41++91EB 3A 85 95             ld      a,(p127_irq_vector_owner)
  42++91EE FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE
  43++91F0 C2 F7 93             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  44++91F3 3A 86 95             ld      a,(p127_irq_mode_policy)
  45++91F6 FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET
  46++91F8 C2 F7 93             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  47++91FB B7                   or      a
  48++91FC C9                   ret
  49++91FD
  50++91FD              ; Fault-injection tests reject enabled-state, vector-owner and guessed-IM claims.
  51++91FD              p127_irq_selftest:

  64++921D D2 F9 93             jp      nc,p127_irq_test_fail

  76++9236              ; Readiness gate only. Carry is set unless vector ownership, mapped table,
  77++9236              ; and resident handler are all explicitly committed. This routine NEVER EI.

 115++9282              ; Stage 2.16 batch: exhaust the complete 3-bit readiness mask and all
 116++9282              ; prerequisite failure paths in one boot. This is a software contract test:
 117++9282              ; it does not model or claim an ATM interrupt source/vector/acknowledge cycle.
 118++9282              ; On success the real boot tuple is restored to DI + no owner + no mode.
 119++9282              p127_irq_batch_selftest:

 197++9313              ; Stage 2.17 — install a deterministic ATM710 IM2 vector while masked.
 198++9313              ; unreal.ini selects FloatBus=0 and FloatDOS=0, so TMainZ80::IntVec()
 199++9313              ; supplies #FF in normal and TR-DOS operation. This routine installs only

 259++9376              ; Stage 2.18 — bounded one-shot live ATM frame IRQ probe.

 364++93FE              ; Stage 2.15: resident interrupt frame protocol.
 365++93FE              ; Hardware vector installation and IM selection are deliberately separate.
 366++93FE              ; Entry contract: CPU has already pushed the interrupted PC; W1/P5 code and
 367++93FE              ; W2/P2 stack remain mapped. The dispatcher may freely clobber all registers.
 368++93FE              ; RETI restores IFF1 from IFF2; this routine never executes EI or IM.

  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret

 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly

 598+ A40C
 599+ A40C
 600+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 601+ A40C              ; Project127 ATM video mode smoke test.
 602+ A40C              ; Reference mode bytes are taken from Project112's tested mode table:
 603+ A40C              ;   RG3 #C3 Sinclair, RG2 #C2 ATM640, RG0 #C0 EGA, RG6 #C6 text.
 604+ A40C              ; W1/W3 are remapped only while the UI owns W3. The trampoline is written
 605+ A40C              ; into the currently mapped video page 3 at #FFEC, then host pages are
 606+ A40C              ; restored immediately after the mode-register write.
 607+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 608+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR EQU #C3
 609+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640   EQU #C2
 610+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA      EQU #C0
 611+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT     EQU #C6
 612+ A40C
 613+ A40C              p127_video_mode_selftest:
 614+ A40C                      ; Preserve representative character and attribute bytes from both
 615+ A40C                      ; parity planes. The mode changes themselves must not alter VRAM.
 616+ A40C AF                   xor     a
 617+ A40D 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 618+ A410 CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 619+ A413 D8                   ret     c
 620+ A414
 621+ A414 3E C3                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR
 622+ A416 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 623+ A419 D8                   ret     c
 624+ A41A CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 625+ A41D
 626+ A41D 3E C2                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640
 627+ A41F CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 628+ A422 D8                   ret     c
 629+ A423 CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 630+ A426
 631+ A426 3E C0                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_EGA
 632+ A428 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 633+ A42B D8                   ret     c
 634+ A42C CD AE A5             call    p127_video_visible_delay
 635+ A42F
 636+ A42F                      ; Always return the controller to the Project127 UI mode.
 637+ A42F 3E C6                ld      a,P127_VIDEO_MODE_TEXT
 638+ A431 CD 76 A4             call    p127_video_switch_mode
 639+ A434 D8                   ret     c
 640+ A435
 641+ A435 3E 01                ld      a,1
 642+ A437 32 C2 A5             ld      (p127_video_capture_phase),a
 643+ A43A CD F0 A4             call    p127_video_capture_signature
 644+ A43D D8                   ret     c
 645+ A43E 3A C3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_even)
 646+ A441 21 C7 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_even
 647+ A444 BE                   cp      (hl)
 648+ A445 20 2D                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 649+ A447 3A C4 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_char_odd)
 650+ A44A 21 C8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_char_odd
 651+ A44D BE                   cp      (hl)
 652+ A44E 20 24                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 653+ A450 3A C5 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_even)
 654+ A453 21 C9 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_even
 655+ A456 BE                   cp      (hl)
 656+ A457 20 1B                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 657+ A459 3A C6 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_sig_attr_odd)
 658+ A45C 21 CA A5             ld      hl,p127_video_sig_after_attr_odd
 659+ A45F BE                   cp      (hl)
 660+ A460 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 661+ A462                      ; Compare complete-page CRCs: P7 character plane and P3 attributes.
 662+ A462 21 D8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_before
 663+ A465 11 DC A5             ld      de,p127_video_crc_after
 664+ A468 06 04                ld      b,4
 665+ A46A              p127_video_crc_compare_loop:
 666+ A46A 1A                   ld      a,(de)
 667+ A46B BE                   cp      (hl)
 668+ A46C 20 06                jr      nz,p127_video_test_fail
 669+ A46E 23                   inc     hl
 670+ A46F 13                   inc     de
 671+ A470 10 F8                djnz    p127_video_crc_compare_loop
 672+ A472 B7                   or      a
 673+ A473 C9                   ret

 675+ A474              p127_video_test_fail:
 676+ A474 37                   scf
 677+ A475 C9                   ret
 678+ A476
 679+ A476              ; IN A=complete ATM mode-register byte. Carry means W3 UI lease refused.
 680+ A476              p127_video_switch_mode:
 681+ A476 32 C1 A5             ld      (p127_video_mode_arg),a
 682+ A479 C5                   push    bc
 683+ A47A D5                   push    de
 684+ A47B E5                   push    hl

 687+ A481 CD F7 A2             call    p126_screen_map_video_pages
 688+ A484
 689+ A484                      ; Preserve the original P3 bytes occupied by the temporary trampoline.
 690+ A484                      ; The trampoline returns to resident code, where these bytes are restored
 691+ A484                      ; before P3 is unmapped. This keeps the full-page CRC genuinely read-only.
 692+ A484 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 693+ A487 11 CB A5             ld      de,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 694+ A48A 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 695+ A48D ED B0                ldir

 697+ A48F                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p127_video_mode_return.
 698+ A48F 21 EC FF             ld      hl,#FFEC
 699+ A492 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 700+ A494 23                   inc     hl
 701+ A495 36 77                ld      (hl),#77
 702+ A497 23                   inc     hl
 703+ A498 36 01                ld      (hl),#01
 704+ A49A 23                   inc     hl
 705+ A49B 36 ED                ld      (hl),#ED
 706+ A49D 23                   inc     hl
 707+ A49E 36 79                ld      (hl),#79
 708+ A4A0 23                   inc     hl

 711+ A4A4 36 AF                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return & #FF
 712+ A4A6 23                   inc     hl
 713+ A4A7 36 A4                ld      (hl),p127_video_mode_return >> 8

 715+ A4AC C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 716+ A4AF
 717+ A4AF              p127_video_mode_return:
 718+ A4AF                      ; P3 is still mapped here: restore the bytes overwritten by the trampoline.
 719+ A4AF 21 CB A5             ld      hl,p127_video_trampoline_saved
 720+ A4B2 11 EC FF             ld      de,#FFEC
 721+ A4B5 01 08 00             ld      bc,8
 722+ A4B8 ED B0                ldir

 724+ A4BD CD BD 70             call    p127_w3_ui_leave
 725+ A4C0
 726+ A4C0                      ; Verify the CPU windows and W3 ownership contract after EVERY mode
 727+ A4C0                      ; write. W2 must be the host page P5, W3 must be P0, and the UI lease
 728+ A4C0                      ; must be released before another mode transition is attempted.
 729+ A4C0 3A 6B 84             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+1)
 730+ A4C3 FE 05                cp      5
 731+ A4C5 20 1F                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 732+ A4C7 3A 6D 84             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 733+ A4CA B7                   or      a
 734+ A4CB 20 19                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 735+ A4CD 3A 2F 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 736+ A4D0 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 737+ A4D2 20 12                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error

 739+ A4D7 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI

 742+ A4DE B7                   or      a
 743+ A4DF 20 05                jr      nz,p127_video_mode_restore_error
 744+ A4E1 E1                   pop     hl
 745+ A4E2 D1                   pop     de
 746+ A4E3 C1                   pop     bc
 747+ A4E4 B7                   or      a
 748+ A4E5 C9                   ret
 749+ A4E6

 751+ A4E6 E1                   pop     hl
 752+ A4E7 D1                   pop     de
 753+ A4E8 C1                   pop     bc
 754+ A4E9 37                   scf
 755+ A4EA C9                   ret
 756+ A4EB

 759+ A4EC D1                   pop     de
 760+ A4ED C1                   pop     bc
 761+ A4EE 37                   scf
 762+ A4EF C9                   ret

 764+ A4F0              ; Capture bytes from both character parity planes and both attribute planes.
 765+ A4F0              ; OUT: before signature when p127_video_capture_target=0, after when =1.
 766+ A4F0              p127_video_capture_signature:
 767+ A4F0 C5                   push    bc
 768+ A4F1 D5                   push    de
 769+ A4F2 E5                   push    hl

 778+ A507 3A C0 61             ld      a,(#61C0)

 783+ A516 32 C6 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_attr_odd),a
 784+ A519 18 18                jr      p127_video_capture_done
 785+ A51B              p127_video_capture_after:
 786+ A51B 3A C0 41             ld      a,(#41C0)
 787+ A51E 32 C7 A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_char_even),a
 788+ A521 3A C0 61             ld      a,(#61C0)

 792+ A52D 3A C1 C1             ld      a,(#C1C1)
 793+ A530 32 CA A5             ld      (p127_video_sig_after_attr_odd),a
 794+ A533              p127_video_capture_done:
 795+ A533                      ; Full-page CRC-16/CCITT over the complete 16 KiB P7 and P3 windows.
 796+ A533                      ; This is read-only: no VRAM byte is overwritten by the test.
 797+ A533 CD 67 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_pages
 798+ A536 3A C2 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_capture_phase)
 799+ A539 B7                   or      a
 800+ A53A 20 05                jr      nz,p127_video_crc_store_after
 801+ A53C 21 D8 A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_before
 802+ A53F 18 03                jr      p127_video_crc_store
 803+ A541              p127_video_crc_store_after:
 804+ A541 21 DC A5             ld      hl,p127_video_crc_after
 805+ A544              p127_video_crc_store:
 806+ A544 3A D4 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p7)
 807+ A547 77                   ld      (hl),a
 808+ A548 23                   inc     hl
 809+ A549 3A D5 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p7+1)
 810+ A54C 77                   ld      (hl),a
 811+ A54D 23                   inc     hl
 812+ A54E 3A D6 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p3)
 813+ A551 77                   ld      (hl),a
 814+ A552 23                   inc     hl
 815+ A553 3A D7 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_p3+1)
 816+ A556 77                   ld      (hl),a

 818+ A55A CD BD 70             call    p127_w3_ui_leave

 820+ A55E D1                   pop     de
 821+ A55F C1                   pop     bc
 822+ A560 B7                   or      a
 823+ A561 C9                   ret
 824+ A562              p127_video_capture_refused:
 825+ A562 E1                   pop     hl
 826+ A563 D1                   pop     de
 827+ A564 C1                   pop     bc
 828+ A565 37                   scf
 829+ A566 C9                   ret
 830+ A567
 831+ A567              ; Calculate CRC-16/CCITT (poly #1021, init #FFFF) over both mapped
 832+ A567              ; 16 KiB pages. W1=#4000..#7FFF is P7; W3=#C000..#FFFF is P3.
 833+ A567              ; OUT: p127_video_crc_p7 and p127_video_crc_p3, low byte first.
 834+ A567              p127_video_crc16_pages:
 835+ A567 C5                   push    bc
 836+ A568 D5                   push    de
 837+ A569 E5                   push    hl
 838+ A56A 21 00 40             ld      hl,#4000
 839+ A56D CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 840+ A570 ED 53 D4 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p7),de
 841+ A574 21 00 C0             ld      hl,#C000
 842+ A577 CD 82 A5             call    p127_video_crc16_page
 843+ A57A ED 53 D6 A5          ld      (p127_video_crc_p3),de
 844+ A57E E1                   pop     hl
 845+ A57F D1                   pop     de
 846+ A580 C1                   pop     bc
 847+ A581 C9                   ret
 848+ A582
 849+ A582              ; IN HL=window base. OUT DE=CRC-16/CCITT.
 850+ A582              p127_video_crc16_page:
 851+ A582 11 FF FF             ld      de,#FFFF
 852+ A585 01 00 40             ld      bc,#4000
 853+ A588              p127_video_crc16_byte:
 854+ A588 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 855+ A589 AA                   xor     d
 856+ A58A 57                   ld      d,a
 857+ A58B 3E 08                ld      a,8
 858+ A58D 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 859+ A590              p127_video_crc16_bit:
 860+ A590 CB 23                sla     e
 861+ A592 CB 12                rl      d
 862+ A594 30 08                jr      nc,p127_video_crc16_no_poly
 863+ A596 7A                   ld      a,d
 864+ A597 EE 10                xor     #10
 865+ A599 57                   ld      d,a
 866+ A59A 7B                   ld      a,e
 867+ A59B EE 21                xor     #21
 868+ A59D 5F                   ld      e,a
 869+ A59E              p127_video_crc16_no_poly:
 870+ A59E 3A D3 A5             ld      a,(p127_video_crc_bits)
 871+ A5A1 3D                   dec     a
 872+ A5A2 32 D3 A5             ld      (p127_video_crc_bits),a
 873+ A5A5 20 E9                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_bit
 874+ A5A7 23                   inc     hl
 875+ A5A8 0B                   dec     bc
 876+ A5A9 78                   ld      a,b
 877+ A5AA B1                   or      c
 878+ A5AB 20 DB                jr      nz,p127_video_crc16_byte
 879+ A5AD C9                   ret

 882+ A5AE              p127_video_visible_delay:

   1+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A5E0              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A5E0              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A5E0              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A5E0              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A5E0              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A5E0              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A5E0              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A5E0
  15+ A5E0              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A5E0              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A5E0              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A5E0              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A5E0              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A5E0              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A5E0              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:

  68+ A642 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)

 224+ A7CC                      ; Stage 2.19: exhaust the allocator into a private page-ID list.

 226+ A7CC                      ; checked against all prior IDs before it is appended.

 294+ A847                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.

 420+ A924              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A924                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.

 442+ A951                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A951                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A951                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.

 447+ A955 C9                   ret
 448+ A956              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A956 37                   scf

 451+ A958
 452+ A958              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A958              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A958              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A958                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A958                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A958                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A958                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A958 CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init

 461+ A95B                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A95B                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.

 512+ A9C4                      ; Put the decisive result in the fixed status panel as well as the
 513+ A9C4                      ; scrolling log. PrintAt addresses rows directly, so this does not
 514+ A9C4                      ; depend on the current scrolling cursor after the long page replay.

 570+ AA8D              p127_physmem_cleanup_error:

 572+ AA8F 32 51 AD             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
 573+ AA92 37                   scf
 574+ AA93 C9                   ret
 575+ AA94
 576+ AA94              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ AA94              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ AA94              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ AA94 21 79 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ AA97 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ AA99 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ AA9B              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ AA9B 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ AA9C FE 06                cp      6
 585+ AA9E 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ AAA0 FE 07                cp      7
 587+ AAA2 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ AAA4 FE 00                cp      0
 589+ AAA6 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ AAA8 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ AAAA 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ AAAC FE 02                cp      2
 593+ AAAE 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ AAB0 FE 03                cp      3
 595+ AAB2 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ AAB4 FE 04                cp      4
 597+ AAB6 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ AAB8 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ AABA 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ AABC 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ AABE              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ AABE C5                   push    bc
 603+ AABF E5                   push    hl
 604+ AAC0 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ AAC1 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ AAC3 21 79 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ AAC6 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ AAC7 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ AAC8 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ AACA E1                   pop     hl
 611+ AACB C1                   pop     bc
 612+ AACC 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ AACE              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ AACE 0C                   inc     c
 615+ AACF 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ AAD1 B7                   or      a
 617+ AAD2 C9                   ret
 618+ AAD3              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ AAD3 37                   scf
 620+ AAD4 C9                   ret
 621+ AAD5
 622+ AAD5              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ AAD5              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ AAD5 3A 52 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ AAD8 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ AAD9 21 5F AD             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ AADC              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ AADC 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ AADD 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ AADE 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ AAE0 C5                   push    bc
 632+ AAE1 E5                   push    hl
 633+ AAE2 21 79 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ AAE5 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ AAE6 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ AAE7 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ AAE9 E1                   pop     hl
 638+ AAEA C1                   pop     bc
 639+ AAEB 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ AAED 23                   inc     hl
 641+ AAEE 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ AAF0 B7                   or      a
 643+ AAF1 C9                   ret
 644+ AAF2              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ AAF2 37                   scf
 646+ AAF3 C9                   ret
 647+ AAF4
 648+ AAF4              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ AAF4              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ AAF4              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ AAF4 21 79 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ AAF7 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ AAF9 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ AAFB              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ AAFB 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ AAFC FE 00                cp      0
 657+ AAFE 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ AB00 FE 01                cp      1
 659+ AB02 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ AB04 FE 02                cp      2
 661+ AB06 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ AB08 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ AB0A 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ AB0C FE 04                cp      4
 665+ AB0E 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ AB10 FE 05                cp      5
 667+ AB12 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ AB14 FE 07                cp      7
 669+ AB16 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ AB18 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ AB19 B7                   or      a
 672+ AB1A 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ AB1C 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ AB1E              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ AB1E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ AB1F FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ AB21 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ AB23              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ AB23 23                   inc     hl
 680+ AB24 0C                   inc     c
 681+ AB25 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ AB27 B7                   or      a
 683+ AB28 C9                   ret
 684+ AB29              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ AB29 37                   scf
 686+ AB2A C9                   ret
 687+ AB2B
 688+ AB2B              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ AB2B              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ AB2B
 691+ AB2B              ; ============================================================================
 692+ AB2B              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ AB2B              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ AB2B              ; Public ABI:
 695+ AB2B              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ AB2B              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ AB2B              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ AB2B              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ AB2B              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ AB2B              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ AB2B              ; ============================================================================
 702+ AB2B              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ AB2B C3 19 73             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ AB2E
 705+ AB2E              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ AB2E 32 8B AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ AB31 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ AB34 B7                   or      a
 709+ AB35 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ AB38 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ AB3B 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ AB3C 3A 6D 84             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ AB3F B8                   cp      b
 714+ AB40 CA E8 AB             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ AB43 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ AB46 C3 2F 73             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ AB49
 718+ AB49              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ AB49              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ AB49 F3                   di
 721+ AB4A 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ AB4D B7                   or      a
 723+ AB4E C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ AB51 3A 6D 84             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ AB54 B7                   or      a
 726+ AB55 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ AB58 CD C2 70             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ AB5B D8                   ret     c
 729+ AB5C 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ AB5E 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ AB61 CD E1 70             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ AB64 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ AB66 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ AB68 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ AB6B 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ AB6E 32 8A AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ AB71 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ AB73 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ AB76 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ AB79 B7                   or      a
 741+ AB7A C9                   ret
 742+ AB7B              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ AB7B CD 09 71             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ AB7E AF                   xor     a
 745+ AB7F 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ AB82 37                   scf
 747+ AB83 C9                   ret
 748+ AB84
 749+ AB84              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ AB84              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ AB84 32 8B AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ AB87 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ AB8A FE 01                cp      1
 754+ AB8C C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ AB8F 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ AB92 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ AB94 D2 E8 AB             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ AB97 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ AB98 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ AB9A 21 79 75             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ AB9D 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ AB9E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ AB9F FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ ABA1 C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ ABA4 3A 8B AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ ABA7 C3 14 AD             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ ABAA
 768+ ABAA              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ ABAA              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ ABAA              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ ABAA 3A 86 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ ABAD FE 01                cp      1
 773+ ABAF C2 E8 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ ABB2 AF                   xor     a
 775+ ABB3 CD 14 AD             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ ABB6 D8                   ret     c
 777+ ABB7 3A 89 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ ABBA B7                   or      a
 779+ ABBB 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ ABBD 3A 8A AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ ABC0 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ ABC3              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ ABC3 3A 88 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ ABC6 B7                   or      a
 785+ ABC7 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ ABC9 CD F5 70             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ ABCC D8                   ret     c
 788+ ABCD AF                   xor     a
 789+ ABCE 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ ABD1              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ ABD1 3A 87 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ ABD4 B7                   or      a
 793+ ABD5 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ ABD7 CD 09 71             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ ABDA D8                   ret     c
 796+ ABDB AF                   xor     a
 797+ ABDC 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ ABDF              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ ABDF AF                   xor     a
 800+ ABE0 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ ABE3 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ ABE6 B7                   or      a
 803+ ABE7 C9                   ret
 804+ ABE8
 805+ ABE8              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ ABE8 37                   scf
 807+ ABE9 C9                   ret
 808+ ABEA
 809+ ABEA              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ ABEA              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ ABEA              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ ABEA AF                   xor     a
 813+ ABEB 32 86 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ ABEE 32 87 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ ABF1 32 88 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ ABF4 32 89 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ ABF7 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ ABF9 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ ABFC D2 84 AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ ABFF CD 2B AB             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AC02 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AC05 32 8C AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AC08 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AC0B 32 8D AC             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AC0E CD 49 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AC11 DA 7E AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AC14                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AC14 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AC16 CD 84 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AC19 D2 7B AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AC1C 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AC1F CD 84 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AC22 DA 7B AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AC25                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AC25 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AC28 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AC2B D2 7B AC             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AC2E 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AC30 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AC33 CD AA AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AC36 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AC39 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AC3C 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AC3D 3A 8D AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AC40 B8                   cp      b
 846+ AC41 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AC44 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 848+ AC47 CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 849+ AC4A DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 850+ AC4D                      ; First-fit must return the same page after release.
 851+ AC4D CD 2B AB             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 852+ AC50 DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 853+ AC53 47                   ld      b,a
 854+ AC54 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 855+ AC57 B8                   cp      b
 856+ AC58 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 857+ AC5B 78                   ld      a,b
 858+ AC5C CD 2E AB             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 859+ AC5F DA 84 AC             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 860+ AC62 3A 2F 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_state)
 861+ AC65 FE 01                cp      P127_W3_STATE_UI_ACTIVE
 862+ AC67 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 863+ AC6A 3A 30 72             ld      a,(p127_w3_owner)
 864+ AC6D FE 01                cp      P127_W3_OWNER_UI
 865+ AC6F C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 866+ AC72 3A 6D 84             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 867+ AC75 B7                   or      a
 868+ AC76 C2 84 AC             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 869+ AC79 B7                   or      a
 870+ AC7A C9                   ret
 871+ AC7B
 872+ AC7B              p127_physmem_api_test_end:
 873+ AC7B CD AA AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 874+ AC7E              p127_physmem_api_test_free:
 875+ AC7E 3A 8C AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)

 895+ AC94              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ AC94              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ AC94              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ AC94              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ AC94 F5                   push    af
 900+ AC95 C5                   push    bc

 952+ ACE0 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 953+ ACE3 ED 79                out     (c),a
 954+ ACE5 C9                   ret

 956+ ACE6              p127_physmem_trace_fill_text:

 957+ ACEA 20 50 41 47

 957+ ACF2 3D 00

 959+ ACF8 46 59 20 50
 959+ ACFC 41 47 45 20
 959+ AD00 49 44 3D 00

 961+ AD08 34 35 36 37
 961+ AD0C 38 39 41 42
 961+ AD10 43 44 45 46
 962+ AD14
 963+ AD14              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AD14              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AD14              p127_physmem_set_w3:
 966+ AD14 32 59 AD             ld      (p127_physmem_page_arg),a
 967+ AD17 CD 8A 82             call    p127_map_encode_page
 968+ AD1A D8                   ret     c
 969+ AD1B 01 F7 FF             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT
 970+ AD1E ED 79                out     (c),a
 971+ AD20 3A 59 AD             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_arg)
 972+ AD23 32 6D 84             ld      (p127_map_shadow+3),a
 973+ AD26 B7                   or      a
 974+ AD27 C9                   ret
 975+ AD28
 976+ AD28              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AD28              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AD28              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AD28 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AD2B 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AD2E              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AD2E 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AD2F 07                   rlca
 984+ AD30 AC                   xor     h
 985+ AD31 AA                   xor     d
 986+ AD32 77                   ld      (hl),a
 987+ AD33 23                   inc     hl
 988+ AD34 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AD35 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AD36 B1                   or      c
 991+ AD37 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AD39 C9                   ret
 993+ AD3A
 994+ AD3A              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AD3A              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:
 996+ AD3A 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 997+ AD3D 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 998+ AD40              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop:
 999+ AD40 7D                   ld      a,l
1000+ AD41 07                   rlca
1001+ AD42 AC                   xor     h
1002+ AD43 AA                   xor     d
1003+ AD44 BE                   cp      (hl)
1004+ AD45 20 08                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail
1005+ AD47 23                   inc     hl
1006+ AD48 0B                   dec     bc
1007+ AD49 78                   ld      a,b
1008+ AD4A B1                   or      c
1009+ AD4B 20 F3                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop
1010+ AD4D B7                   or      a
1011+ AD4E C9                   ret
1012+ AD4F              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail:
1013+ AD4F 37                   scf
1014+ AD50 C9                   ret
